# 2027 Big Tech Internships & Graduate Roles

Every open internship and graduate role at 56 big tech companies, read from each firm's own careers page every six hours. Roles are sorted by what the job is and where it is: discipline, then focus, with every city the firm lists. Salary is the base pay a posting states, per year in USD (hourly and monthly pay multiplied out); in practice that means US roles, where the law requires it.

From **[QuantScraper](https://quant-scraper.com/big-tech?for=students)**, where you can filter these by city, firm and focus, and see experienced roles too. Also: [2027 Quant Internships & Graduate Roles](https://github.com/quant-scraper/Quant-Student-Roles) · [2027 AI/ML Internships & Graduate Roles](https://github.com/quant-scraper/AI-Student-Roles).

**1,424** internships · **675** graduate roles · updated 2026-10-29 11:33 UTC
